- **retry**: Send a stopped task (blocked, failed, no response) out again (--by NAME, --delegated WHY, or left out: signed by the session — a retry is not a gate); the next call receives the earlier attempts. --requires: the sandbox lacked CAP — the task says so, and goes to the lock's &lt;role&gt;@&lt;cap&gt;. A session task is measured again and what its measure leaves out is named, with the adopt that settles it. --checked / --checked-red: evidence chongdae runs itself (a pass; a tests task's expected red). A --checked-red run that never reached its tests is refused. ([guinjaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaakeop/chongdae](https://github.com/guinjaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaakeop/chongdae/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/retry.md) · ★1)

- **rev-list**: Enumerate the objects reachable from `wants` and not reachable from `not` — the reachability core of git's `git rev-list`. Walks commits (and, with `objects`, their trees and blobs), returning structured data only; no `--pretty`/`--format`/`--date`/`--abbrev`/`--header`/`-z`/`--object-names` — those are all presentation, left to the caller.
